Theorem 221.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: For any particle_physics module M satisfying the convergence analysis hypothesis, the class [M] in K_0(R) has order dividing 540. The projective resolution has length at most 750 with Betti numbers bounded by 2.

Proof: The proof follows by induction on the dimension. The base case of dimension 540 is immediate from the convergence analysis property. For the inductive step, we decompose the particle_physics structure using the convergence analysis theorem, obtaining subproblems of dimension at most 750. Each subproblem has a solution by the convergence analysis fixed-point theorem with constant 2. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result. QED.
